+"""The hybrid pieces: monitor-written prompts, /reset first and after Dream's IK error, gripper sensors over the
+images, tower prompts with <top>, the operator talking in plain words, and plans/hybrid_v4.yaml."""
+
+import json
+from pathlib import Path
+
+from conftest import stage
+from test_v2 import Script, Transport, build, events, mv, prompts, reply_text, routes
+
+from harness.log import RunLog
+from harness.operator import Operator, load_lessons, parse_reply
+from harness.plan import load_plan
+from harness.runner import AUTO_RESET_MAX, Runner
+from harness.verifiers import MonitorVerifier, parse_monitor_reply
+
+ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parent.parent
+
+
+def test_hybrid_plan_loads_and_cable_section_is_optional():
+    plan = load_plan(ROOT / "plans/hybrid_v4.yaml")
+    names = [s.name for s in plan.stages]
+    assert plan.reset_first and plan.rewrite_prompts and plan.system_prompt and plan.time_budget_s == 295
+    assert names[0] == "cable_unplug" and "cable_extra_4_down" in names and "stack_2" in names and names[-1].startswith("ball_")
+    assert all(s.prompt.startswith(plan.prompt_prefix) for s in plan.stages if s.name.startswith("cable_"))
+    assert not any(n.startswith("cable_") for n in names[names.index("arms_clear"):])
+    assert all(s.stall_s and s.stall_s >= 30 for s in plan.stages if s.name.startswith(("stack", "tower")))
+    assert "{" not in plan.system_prompt and "orange ball" in plan.stages[-1].prompt
+
+
+def test_next_prompt_parsed_and_asked_for(write_plan):
+    v = parse_monitor_reply(reply_text(next_prompt="  pick up the pink cube\n and place it gently "), 0)
+    assert v.next_prompt == "pick up the pink cube and place it gently"
+    plan = load_plan(write_plan([stage("a", verifier="monitor")], rewrite_prompts=True, system_prompt="lessons"))
+    t = Transport(reply_text())
+    m = MonitorVerifier(plan, transport=t)
+    m.check_now(plan.stages[0], {"video.scene": b"\xff\xd8x\xff\xd9"}, 1.0)
+    assert t.payloads[0]["system"] == "lessons"
+    assert "next_prompt" in t.payloads[0]["messages"][0]["content"][-1]["text"]
+
+
+def test_monitor_rewrites_the_prompt_with_prefix_cooldown_and_cap(fake, write_plan, tmp_path, monkeypatch):
+    monkeypatch.setattr("harness.runner.REWRITE_COOLDOWN_S", 0.0)
+    pre = "No power here."
+    path = write_plan([stage("a", prompt=f"{pre} pull the plug", timeout_s=3.0, max_attempts=1, on_fail="skip")],
+                      rewrite_prompts=True, prompt_prefix=pre)
+    sv = Script([mv(None, "ok", next_prompt="ignored: status ok"), mv(None, "stalled", next_prompt="grip the rightmost plug"),
+                 mv(None, "stalled", next_prompt="grip the second plug"),
+                 mv(None, "failing", next_prompt="grip the third plug"), mv(None, "stalled", next_prompt="grip a fourth plug"),
+                 mv(True, progress=1.0)], repeat=True)
+    r = build(fake, path, tmp_path, sv)
+    r.run()
+    sent = prompts(fake)
+    rewrites = events(r.log, "prompt_rewrite")
+    assert len(rewrites) == 3                                   # REWRITE_MAX per attempt
+    assert sent[1] == f"{pre} grip the rightmost plug"          # the prefix is put back on
+    assert all(e["previous"] for e in rewrites)
+
+
+def test_reset_first_then_auto_reset_after_svd(fake, write_plan, tmp_path):
+    fake.reset_s = fake.home_s = 0.05
+    path = write_plan([stage("a", timeout_s=6.0)], reset_first=True)
+    hits = {"n": 0}
+
+    def svd(st, frames, elapsed):
+        hits["n"] += 1
+        if hits["n"] in (2, 6, 10):
+            with fake._lock:
+                fake.last_error = "LinAlgError: SVD did not converge"
+                fake.state = "error"
+        return mv(True, progress=1.0) if hits["n"] > 14 else mv(None)
+
+    r = build(fake, path, tmp_path, Script([svd]))
+    r.run()
+    rs = routes(fake)
+    assert rs[0] == "/reset" and rs[1] == "/start"              # grippers open before the first stage
+    assert len(events(r.log, "auto_reset")) == AUTO_RESET_MAX   # a third SVD restarts without a reset
+    assert rs.count("/reset") == 1 + AUTO_RESET_MAX
+
+
+def test_gripper_note_reads_this_stations_sensors(fake, write_plan, tmp_path):
+    r = build(fake, write_plan([stage("a")]), tmp_path, Script([mv(None)]))
+    r.station.grippers = lambda: {"right": {"gripper": 0.03, "commanded_gripper": None},
+                                  "left": {"gripper": 0.11, "commanded_gripper": 0.0}}
+    note = r._grip_note()
+    assert "right gripper shut on nothing" in note and "left gripper nearly shut (0.11): a thin plug or nothing" in note
+
+
+def test_done_seen_during_recovery_passes_after_a_second_look(fake, write_plan, tmp_path):
+    rec = [{"name": "lift", "kind": "reset", "prompt": "open and lift", "settle_s": 0.05}]
+    path = write_plan([stage("a", timeout_s=0.3, max_attempts=3, recovery=rec)])
+    sv = Script([mv(None, "ok")])
+    r = build(fake, path, tmp_path, sv)
+    r._reset_scene = lambda st, rc, v: (setattr(r, "_done_in_recovery", "plug out"), (True, "scene ok"))[1]
+    sv.check_now = lambda st, frames, e: mv(True, progress=1.0)
+    res = r.run()
+    assert res.stages[0].ok and res.stages[0].reason == "done during recovery: plug out"
+    assert events(r.log, "recovery_done_check")[0]["passed"]
+
+
+def test_tower_prompt_names_the_piece_below(fake, write_plan, tmp_path):
+    path = write_plan([stage("stack_2")], tower_target=["blue cube", "pink cube", "yellow stick"],
+                      tower_prompts={"cube": "put the <piece> on the <top>", "stick": "stand the <piece> on the <top>"})
+    r = build(fake, path, tmp_path, Script([mv(None)]))
+    r._tower = ["blue cube", "pink cube"]
+    assert r._stage_prompt(r.plan.stages[0], None) == "stand the yellow stick on the pink cube"

+def test_operator_reply_parsing():
+    text = 'Skipping it.\n<json>{"reply": "ok, skipping stack_3", "skip_stages": ["stack_3", "nope"], ' \
+           '"end_current_stage": false, "prompt_now": null, "note": "lower blocks slowly"}</json>'
+    reply, acts = parse_reply(text, ["stack_2", "stack_3"])
+    assert reply == "ok, skipping stack_3"
+    assert acts == {"skip_stages": ["stack_3"], "note": "lower blocks slowly"}
+    assert parse_reply("just words", ["a"]) == ("just words", {})

+def test_operator_skips_ends_and_notes(fake, write_plan, tmp_path):
+    path = write_plan([stage("a", timeout_s=5.0), stage("b"), stage("c", timeout_s=0.5)])
+    log = RunLog(tmp_path / "runs")
+    plan = load_plan(path)
+    lessons = tmp_path / "lessons.md"
+    t = Transport('<json>{"reply": "ok", "end_current_stage": true, "skip_stages": ["b"], '
+                  '"note": "carry blocks high"}</json>')
+    op = Operator(plan, log, transport=t, lessons_path=lessons, print_fn=lambda s: None)
+    log.listeners.append(op.record)
+    notes = []
+
+    class V(Script):
+        operator_notes = notes
+
+    sv = V([lambda st, f, e: (op.submit("skip the next step and stop this one") if st.name == "a" and len(sv.calls) == 2
+                              else None) or mv(None)])
+    r = build(fake, path, tmp_path, sv, log=log)
+    r.operator = op
+    res = r.run()
+    by = {s.name: s for s in res.stages}
+    assert by["a"].reason == "operator ended the stage" and by["b"].reason == "skipped by operator"
+    assert not any(c[0] == "b" for c in sv.calls) and any(c[0] == "c" for c in sv.calls)
+    assert notes == ["carry blocks high"]
+    assert "carry blocks high" in load_lessons(lessons)
+    kinds = [e["event"] for e in events(log)]
+    assert "operator_input" in kinds and "operator_reply" in kinds and "operator_action" in kinds
+    assert "skip the next step" in t.payloads[0]["messages"][0]["content"][-1]["text"]
+
+
+def test_trace_report_shows_rewrites_and_operator(tmp_path):
+    import sys
+    sys.path.insert(0, str(ROOT / "tools"))
+    import trace_report
+    run = tmp_path / "20261107T000000Z"
+    run.mkdir()
+    evs = [{"t": 0, "event": "run_start", "task": "t"},
+           {"t": 1, "event": "prompt_rewrite", "stage": "a", "prompt": "new one", "previous": "old one", "reason": "r"},
+           {"t": 2, "event": "operator_input", "text": "why?"},
+           {"t": 3, "event": "operator_reply", "reply": "because", "actions": {}}]
+    (run / "events.jsonl").write_text("\n".join(json.dumps(e) for e in evs))
+    trace_report.render(run)
+    page = (run / "trace.html").read_text()
+    assert "monitor rewrote prompt" in page and "class='was'" in page and "operator said" in page
+
+
+def _jpeg(color):
+    import io
+    from PIL import Image
+    out = io.BytesIO()
+    Image.new("RGB", (640, 360), color).save(out, "JPEG")
+    return out.getvalue()
+
+
+def test_stitched_history_and_two_calls_in_flight(write_plan):
+    import threading
+    plan = load_plan(write_plan([stage("a", verifier="monitor")], claude={"stitch": True, "parallel": 2,
+                                                                        "history_frames": 4}))
+    gate, started = threading.Event(), []
+
+    def slow(payload):
+        started.append(payload)
+        gate.wait(5)
+        return {"content": [{"type": "text", "text": reply_text(progress=len(started) / 10)}]}
+
+    m = MonitorVerifier(plan, transport=slow)
+    st = plan.stages[0]
+    for t in range(4):
+        m.check(st, {"video.scene": _jpeg((t * 40, 0, 0)), "video.wrist_r": _jpeg("blue")}, float(t))
+    assert len(started) == 2                       # at most two in flight
+    gate.set()
+    m.wait_idle(3)
+    m.check(st, {"video.scene": _jpeg("red"), "video.wrist_r": _jpeg("blue")}, 5.0)
+    m.wait_idle(3)
+    last = started[-1]["messages"][0]["content"]
+    images = [c for c in last if c["type"] == "image"]
+    texts = [c["text"] for c in last if c["type"] == "text"]
+    assert len(images) == 3 and texts[0].startswith("Earlier frames in ONE image")   # grid + newest scene + wrist
+    v = m.check(st, {"video.scene": _jpeg("red")}, 6.0)
+    assert v.latency_s is not None
+
+
+def test_put_down_skipped_when_its_unplug_failed(fake, write_plan, tmp_path):
+    path = write_plan([stage("up", timeout_s=0.3, max_attempts=1, on_fail="skip"),
+                       stage("down", requires_stage="up"), stage("next", timeout_s=0.3)])

+    assert by["down"].reason == "up did not pass" and not any(c[0] == "down" for c in sv.calls)
+

+"""LiveKit observer: the parts that run without the livekit package (freshness gate, argument checks)."""
+
+from __future__ import annotations
+
+import pytest
+
+from harness import livekit_observer as lko
+
+
+class _Room:
+    def __init__(self, connected: bool = True) -> None:
+        self.connected = connected
+
+    def isconnected(self) -> bool:
+        return self.connected
+
+
+def _cameras(latest: dict, roles=("overhead", "wrist_l"), connected=True, reconnecting=False):
+    cams = object.__new__(lko.LiveKitCameras)      # skip __init__: it needs the livekit SDK
+    cams.room = _Room(connected)
+    cams._roles = {f"track_{r}": r for r in roles}
+    cams._latest = latest
+    cams._reconnecting = reconnecting
+    return cams
+
+
+def _frame(t: float) -> lko.ReceivedFrame:
+    return lko.ReceivedFrame(event=None, received_at=t)
+
+
+def test_sample_needs_every_view_fresh_and_new():
+    a, b = _frame(9.5), _frame(9.8)
+    cams = _cameras({"overhead": a, "wrist_l": b})
+    assert cams.sample(10.0, 1.0, {}) == {"overhead": a, "wrist_l": b}
+    assert cams.sample(10.0, 1.0, {"overhead": a}) == {}          # one view unchanged since last sample
+    assert cams.sample(11.0, 1.0, {}) == {}                       # overhead is 1.5 s old
+    assert _cameras({"overhead": a}).sample(10.0, 1.0, {}) == {}  # wrist missing
+
+
+def test_sample_is_empty_while_disconnected_or_reconnecting():
+    latest = {"overhead": _frame(9.9), "wrist_l": _frame(9.9)}
+    assert _cameras(dict(latest), connected=False).sample(10.0, 1.0, {}) == {}
+    assert _cameras(dict(latest), reconnecting=True).sample(10.0, 1.0, {}) == {}
+
+
+def test_camera_names_match_station_aliases():
+    assert lko.camera_matches("video.top", "overhead")
+    assert lko.camera_matches("left", "wrist_l")
+    assert not lko.camera_matches("right", "wrist_l")
+
+
+@pytest.mark.parametrize("argv, msg", [
+    (["--publisher", "p", "--track", "ceiling=x"], "unique overhead"),
+    (["--publisher", "p", "--track", "overhead=x", "--track", "wrist_l=x"], "unique overhead"),
+    (["--publisher", "p", "--track", "overhead=x", "--plan", "plans/hill_climb_v4.yaml"], "used together"),
+    (["--publisher", "p", "--track", "overhead=x"], "LIVEKIT_URL"),
+])
+def test_bad_arguments_are_refused(argv, msg, monkeypatch, capsys):
+    monkeypatch.delenv("LIVEKIT_URL", raising=False)
+    monkeypatch.delenv("LIVEKIT_TOKEN", raising=False)
+    with pytest.raises(SystemExit) as exc:
+        lko.main(argv)
+    assert exc.value.code == 2
+    assert msg in capsys.readouterr().err
+
+
+def test_seconds_must_be_positive():
+    import argparse
+    with pytest.raises(argparse.ArgumentTypeError):
+        lko.positive_seconds("0")
+    assert lko.positive_seconds("2.5") == 2.5

+"""plan.start_pose (home before a stage when the arms start far from every recorded start) and the live-frame guard."""
+
+import json
+import threading
+
+import pytest
+
+from conftest import stage, wait_until
+from test_v2 import Script, build, events, mv, routes
+from harness.live_guard import LiveFrames, LiveGuard
+from harness.plan import parse_plan
+from harness.start_pose import StartPoses, joints_from_arms, joints_from_observation
+
+HOME = [0.0] * 6
+RAISED = [0.3, -1.7, 0.75, 0.1, 0.05, -0.1]
+
+
+def _ref(**by_prompt):
+    return StartPoses({p: [{"left": l, "right": r, "episode": f"ep{i}"} for i, (l, r) in enumerate(starts)]
+                       for p, starts in by_prompt.items()})
+
+
+# -- start pose: the check --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
+def test_check_statuses():
+    ref = _ref(**{"do unplug": [(HOME, HOME)]})
+    near = {"left": HOME, "right": [0.1, 0, 0, 0, 0, 0]}
+    assert ref.check("do unplug", near, 0.35).status == "ok"
+    far = ref.check("do unplug", {"left": HOME, "right": RAISED}, 0.35)
+    assert far.status == "far" and far.distance["right"] == pytest.approx(1.7) and far.nearest == "ep0"
+    assert ref.check("other prompt", near, 0.35).status == "no_reference"
+    assert ref.check("do unplug", None, 0.35).status == "no_state"
+
+
+def test_nearest_start_wins():
+    ref = _ref(p=[(HOME, HOME), (HOME, RAISED)])
+    chk = ref.check("p", {"left": HOME, "right": [0.25, -1.6, 0.7, 0.1, 0.0, -0.1]}, 0.35)
+    assert chk.status == "ok" and chk.nearest == "ep1"
+
+
+def test_joint_sources():
+    assert joints_from_arms({"left": {"gripper": 1.0}, "right": {"gripper": 1.0}}) is None   # old add-on
+    assert joints_from_arms({"right": {"positions_rad": RAISED}}) == {"right": RAISED}
+    assert joints_from_observation({"state.left_arm": HOME, "state.right_arm": RAISED, "prompt": "x"}) == \
+        {"left": HOME, "right": RAISED}
+    assert joints_from_observation({"prompt": "x"}) is None
+
+
+# -- start pose: the plan ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
+def _plan(**sp):
+    return parse_plan({"task": "t", "start_pose": {"reference": "r.json", **sp},
+                       "stages": [{"name": "unplug_cable", "prompt": "u", "success": "u?"}]})
+
+
+def test_start_pose_parses():
+    sp = _plan(home_before=["unplug"], tolerance_rad=0.2).start_pose
+    assert sp.homes("unplug_cable") and sp.tolerance_rad == 0.2 and sp.max_homes == 1 and sp.home_pose["left"] == HOME
+
+
+@pytest.mark.parametrize("sp, match", [
+    ({"home_before": ["stack"]}, "matches no stage"),
+    ({"home_pose": {"left": HOME}}, "home_pose"),
+    ({"tolerance_rad": 0}, "tolerance_rad"),
+    ({"homes": 2}, "unknown key"),
+])
+def test_bad_start_pose_rejected(sp, match):
+    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match=match):
+        _plan(**sp)
+

+# -- start pose: the runner -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
+def _pose_runner(fake, write_plan, tmp_path, *, right, home_before=("unplug_cable",), refs=None, verdicts=None,
+                 max_attempts=2):
+    path = write_plan([stage("unplug_cable", prompt="do unplug", max_attempts=max_attempts, timeout_s=0.6)],
+                      start_pose={"reference": "unused.json", "home_before": list(home_before)}, time_budget_s=300)
+    runner = build(fake, path, tmp_path, Script(verdicts or [mv(True)]))
+    runner.start_poses = refs or _ref(**{"do unplug": [(HOME, HOME)]})
+    runner.station.grippers = lambda: {"left": {"positions_rad": HOME}, "right": {"positions_rad": right}}
+    return runner
+
+
+def test_far_start_homes_before_the_first_prompt(fake, write_plan, tmp_path):
+    runner = _pose_runner(fake, write_plan, tmp_path, right=RAISED)
+    assert runner.run().ok
+    r = routes(fake)
+    assert r.index("/home") < r.index("/start")
+    ev = events(runner.log, "start_pose")[0]
+    assert ev["status"] == "far" and ev["source"] == "arms" and ev["why"] == "stage start"
+    assert events(runner.log, "start_pose_home")[0]["done"] is True
+
+
+def test_near_start_does_not_home(fake, write_plan, tmp_path):
+    runner = _pose_runner(fake, write_plan, tmp_path, right=HOME)
+    assert runner.run().ok
+    assert "/home" not in routes(fake)
+    assert events(runner.log, "start_pose")[0]["status"] == "ok"
+
+
+def test_other_stages_only_log(fake, write_plan, tmp_path):
+    runner = _pose_runner(fake, write_plan, tmp_path, right=RAISED, home_before=())
+    assert runner.run().ok
+    assert "/home" not in routes(fake) and events(runner.log, "start_pose")[0]["status"] == "far"
+

+def test_no_home_when_home_is_not_a_recorded_start(fake, write_plan, tmp_path):

+    runner = _pose_runner(fake, write_plan, tmp_path, right=RAISED, refs=refs)
+    assert runner.run().ok
+    assert "/home" not in routes(fake)

+def test_max_homes_per_stage(fake, write_plan, tmp_path):
+    runner = _pose_runner(fake, write_plan, tmp_path, right=RAISED, verdicts=[mv(False, "failing")], max_attempts=3)
+    runner.run()
+    assert routes(fake).count("/home") == 1          # stage start homed; the retries only logged
+    homes = events(runner.log, "start_pose_home")
+    assert homes[0]["done"] is True and all(h["done"] is False for h in homes[1:]) and len(homes) >= 2

+def test_retry_after_far_start_homes_then_starts_fresh(fake, write_plan, tmp_path):
+    arms = {"right": HOME, "failed": False}
+    verdict = Script([lambda st, f, e: mv(True) if arms["failed"] else mv(False, "failing")])
+    runner = _pose_runner(fake, write_plan, tmp_path, right=HOME, verdicts=None)
+    runner.verifiers = {k: verdict for k in runner.verifiers}
+    runner.station.grippers = lambda: {"left": {"positions_rad": HOME}, "right": {"positions_rad": arms["right"]}}
+    orig = runner._attempt
+
+    def attempt(*a, **k):
+        out = orig(*a, **k)
+        arms["right"], arms["failed"] = RAISED, True   # the failed attempt left the arm up over the hub
+        return out
+    runner._attempt = attempt
+    assert runner.run().ok
+    r = routes(fake)
+    assert r.count("/home") == 1 and r.count("/start") == 2 and r.index("/home") < len(r) - 1
+    assert [e["mode"] for e in events(runner.log, "retry")] == ["home_then_retry"]

+def test_no_reference_file_means_no_checks(fake, write_plan, tmp_path):
+    runner = _pose_runner(fake, write_plan, tmp_path, right=RAISED)
+    runner.start_poses = None
+    assert runner.run().ok and not events(runner.log, "start_pose")

+def test_reference_tool(tmp_path):
+    pa = pytest.importorskip("pyarrow")
+    pq = pytest.importorskip("pyarrow.parquet")
+    from tools.start_poses import main as build_ref
+    ds = tmp_path / "set"
+    (ds / "meta").mkdir(parents=True)
+    (ds / "data" / "chunk-000").mkdir(parents=True)
+    names = [f"left_joint_{j}" for j in range(6)] + ["left_gripper"] + [f"right_joint_{j}" for j in range(6)] + \
+        ["right_gripper"]
+    (ds / "meta" / "info.json").write_text(json.dumps({"features": {"observation.state": {"names": names}}}))
+    (ds / "meta" / "tasks.jsonl").write_text('{"task_index": 0, "task": "a"}\n{"task_index": 1, "task": "b"}\n')
+    rows = [[0.0] * 7 + [0.1] * 7, [0.0] * 7 + [0.2] * 7, [0.0] * 7 + [0.9] * 7]
+    pq.write_table(pa.table({"observation.state": rows, "task_index": [0, 0, 1]}),
+                   ds / "data" / "chunk-000" / "episode_000000.parquet")
+    out = tmp_path / "ref.json"
+    assert build_ref([str(out), str(ds)]) == 0
+    ref = StartPoses.load(out)
+    assert ref.by_prompt["a"][0]["right"] == [0.1] * 6 and ref.by_prompt["b"][0]["right"] == [0.9] * 6
+    assert ref.by_prompt["b"][0]["episode"] == "set/episode_000000/seg1"

+# -- live guard -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
+class _Feed:
+    """Distinct frames each grab unless frozen."""
+
+    def __init__(self):
+        self.n, self.frozen = 0, False
+
+    def __call__(self):
+        if not self.frozen:
+            self.n += 1
+        return {"wrist_r": b"jpeg%d" % self.n}
+
+
+def _guard(answers, confirm=3):
+    stops, log = [], []
+    seq = list(answers)
+    g = LiveGuard(_Feed(), lambda f: seq.pop(0) if seq else (False, "no"), lambda: stops.append(1),
+                  confirm_frames=confirm, log=lambda name, **k: log.append((name, k)))
+    return g, stops, log
+
+
+def test_live_guard_needs_confirm_distinct_yes_in_a_row():
+    g, stops, log = _guard([(True, "y"), (False, "n"), (True, "y"), (None, "?"), (True, "y"), (True, "y")])
+    g.step()                                   # disarmed: nothing is looked at
+    assert g.ticks == 0
+    g.arm("ball_1")
+    for _ in range(6):
+        g.step()
+    assert g.tripped == "y" and stops == [1]
+    assert [k["count"] for n, k in log if n == "guard_yes"] == [1, 1, 2, 3]
+    g.step()
+    assert stops == [1]                        # stops once
+
+
+def test_live_guard_frozen_frames_count_once():
+    g, stops, _ = _guard([(True, "y")] * 5)
+    g.arm("ball_1")
+    g.source.frozen = True
+    for _ in range(5):
+        g.step()
+    assert g.tripped is None and not stops
+
+
+def test_live_guard_answer_time_and_disarm():
+    t = [100.0]
+    g = LiveGuard(_Feed(), lambda f: (False, "n"), lambda: None, clock=lambda: t[0])
+    g.arm("ball_1")
+    assert g.last_answer_t == 100.0
+    t[0] = 105.0
+    g.step()
+    assert g.last_answer_t == 105.0
+    g.disarm()
+    t[0] = 110.0
+    g.step()
+    assert g.last_answer_t == 105.0
+
+
+def test_live_frames_probe_finds_no_route(fake):
+    lf = LiveFrames(fake.api_url, ("wrist_l", "wrist_r"))
+    assert lf.probe() == [] and "404" in lf.last_error
+    assert lf.roles == ["wrist_l", "wrist_r"]          # nothing dropped: a camera that comes back is used again
+
+
+# -- Codex review findings ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
+def test_yes_counts_per_camera_not_across_cameras():
+    n = {"l": 0}
+
+    def feed():
+        n["l"] += 1
+        return {"wrist_l": b"moving%d" % n["l"], "wrist_r": b"frozen-yes"}
+    stops = []
+    g = LiveGuard(feed, lambda f: (True, "y") if "wrist_r" in f else (False, "n"), lambda: stops.append(1))
+    g.arm("ball_1")
+    for _ in range(6):
+        g.step()
+    assert g.tripped is None and not stops            # one frozen yes frame is one look
+
+
+def test_frozen_frames_do_not_refresh_the_blind_clock():
+    t = [100.0]
+    g = LiveGuard(lambda: {"wrist_r": b"same"}, lambda f: (False, "n"), lambda: None, clock=lambda: t[0])
+    g.arm("ball_1")
+    t[0] = 101.0
+    g.step()
+    assert g.last_answer_t == 101.0
+    t[0] = 130.0
+    g.step()
+    assert g.last_answer_t == 101.0                   # max_blind_s will see the camera as blind
+
+
+def test_a_judgment_from_before_a_rearm_is_dropped():
+    g = None
+    stops = []
+
+    def judge(f):
+        g.disarm()
+        g.arm("ball_2")                               # the runner moved on while this frame was being judged
+        return True, "y"
+    g = LiveGuard(_Feed(), judge, lambda: stops.append(1), confirm_frames=1)
+    g.arm("ball_1")
+    g.step()
+    assert g.tripped is None and not stops
+
+
+def test_no_motion_command_after_a_trip(fake, write_plan, tmp_path):
+    from harness.runner import _Guard
+    runner = build(fake, write_plan([stage("ball_1")]), tmp_path, Script([mv(True)]))
+    g = LiveGuard(_Feed(), lambda f: (True, "y"), lambda: None, confirm_frames=1)
+    runner.live_guard = g
+    g.arm("ball_1")
+    g.step()
+    assert g.tripped
+    before = routes(fake)
+    with pytest.raises(_Guard):
+        runner._motion(runner.station.start, "do ball_1", None)
+    with pytest.raises(_Guard):
+        runner._issue("do ball_1", "stage")
+    assert routes(fake) == before                     # nothing was sent
+

+def test_no_home_without_time_reserve(fake, write_plan, tmp_path):

+    runner.plan.time_budget_s = 20.0                  # < HOME_RESERVE_S
+    runner.run()
+    assert "/home" not in routes(fake)
+    assert "can't be cancelled" in events(runner.log, "start_pose_home")[0]["reason"]
+

+def test_operator_stop_during_the_stop_wait_means_no_home(fake, write_plan, tmp_path):
+    from harness.runner import _Stop
+    runner = _pose_runner(fake, write_plan, tmp_path, right=RAISED)
+    runner._t0 = runner._clock()
+    stage0 = runner.plan.stages[0]
+    runner._issue("do unplug", "stage", force_start=True)          # running, so the gate must /stop first
+    orig_stop = runner.station.stop
+    runner.station.stop = lambda: (runner.request_stop(), orig_stop())[1]
+    with pytest.raises(_Stop):
+        runner._start_pose_gate(stage0, "do unplug", "retry")
+    assert "/home" not in routes(fake)
+
+
+def test_a_trip_ends_a_wait_for_running_at_once(fake, write_plan, tmp_path):
+    import time as _t
+    from harness.runner import _Guard
+    runner = build(fake, write_plan([stage("ball_1")]), tmp_path, Script([mv(True)]))
+    runner.live_guard = type("G", (), {"tripped": "white ball", "last_answer_t": None})()
+    t0 = _t.monotonic()
+    with pytest.raises(_Guard, match="while waiting for running"):
+        runner._wait({"running"}, 30.0, "running")       # the fake is idle: without the trip this waits 30 s
+    assert _t.monotonic() - t0 < 2.0
+
+
+def test_live_guard_trip_ends_the_match_mid_stage(fake, write_plan, tmp_path):
+    q = "Is a gripper holding a black ball?"
+    path = write_plan([stage("stack", timeout_s=0.3, on_fail="skip"), stage("ball_1", timeout_s=5.0, on_fail="skip"),
+                       stage("ball_2", timeout_s=0.3, on_fail="skip")],
+                      verifier="monitor", guard={"question": q, "stages": ["ball_"]})
+    runner = build(fake, path, tmp_path, Script([mv(None, guard=False)]))
+    armed = threading.Event()
+    g = LiveGuard(_Feed(), lambda f: (True, "white ball between the fingers"), runner.station.stop, rate_hz=50,
+                  confirm_frames=3, log=runner.log.event)
+    orig_arm = g.arm
+    g.arm = lambda s: (orig_arm(s), armed.set())
+    runner.live_guard = g
+    g.start()
+    try:
+        result = runner.run()
+    finally:
+        g.close()
+    assert armed.is_set()
+    assert not result.ok and result.guard_tripped and "live frames" in result.reason
+    assert [r.name for r in result.stages] == ["stack"]
+    trip = [e for e in events(runner.log, "guard_tripped") if e.get("source") == "live"]
+    assert trip and trip[0]["stage"] == "ball_1" and trip[0]["stop"] == "sent"
+    assert routes(fake).count("/stop") >= 1
+    assert not wait_until(lambda: g.ticks > 10_000, timeout=0.05)   # thread is idle after the trip
+
+
+# -- second Codex review -----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
+def test_trip_during_a_motion_command_stops_again(fake, write_plan, tmp_path):
+    from harness.runner import _Guard
+    runner = build(fake, write_plan([stage("ball_1")]), tmp_path, Script([mv(True)]))
+    runner._t0 = runner._clock()
+    g = LiveGuard(_Feed(), lambda f: (True, "y"), lambda: None, confirm_frames=1)
+    runner.live_guard = g
+    g.arm("ball_1")
+
+    def slow_start(prompt, checkpoint):
+        g.step()                                       # the guard trips while this POST is in flight
+        return runner.station.start(prompt, checkpoint)
+    with pytest.raises(_Guard):
+        runner._motion(slow_start, "do ball_1", None)
+    r = routes(fake)
+    assert r[-2:] == ["/start", "/stop"]               # the start that overtook the trip is stopped
+
+
+def test_guard_trip_does_not_wait_for_the_runner():
+    stops = []
+    g = LiveGuard(_Feed(), lambda f: (True, "y"), lambda: stops.append(1), confirm_frames=1)
+    assert not hasattr(g, "command_lock")
+    g.arm("ball_1")
+    g.step()
+    assert stops == [1]
+

+def test_per_poll_guard_counts_new_frames_only(fake, write_plan, tmp_path):
+    q = "Is a gripper holding a black ball?"
+    path = write_plan([stage("ball_1", timeout_s=1.5, on_fail="skip")], verifier="monitor",
+                      guard={"question": q, "stages": ["ball_"], "confirm": 2})
+    runner = build(fake, path, tmp_path, Script([mv(None)]))
+    runner.frames.latest = lambda: {"wrist_r": b"the-same-cached-trip"}
+    runner.fast_guard = lambda f: (True, "white ball")
+    result = runner.run()
+    assert not result.guard_tripped                    # one frame, judged once: never two yes answers
+    assert len([e for e in events(runner.log, "guard_yes") if e.get("source") == "local"]) == 1
+

+# -- third Codex review ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
+def test_no_home_while_the_live_guard_is_armed(fake, write_plan, tmp_path):
+    from harness.runner import _Fail
+    runner = build(fake, write_plan([stage("ball_1")]), tmp_path, Script([mv(True)]))
+    runner._t0 = runner._clock()
+    g = LiveGuard(_Feed(), lambda f: (False, "n"), lambda: None)
+    runner.live_guard = g
+    g.arm("ball_1")
+    with pytest.raises(_Fail, match="armed"):
+        runner._motion(runner.station.home)
+    assert "/home" not in routes(fake)
+    g.disarm()
+    runner._motion(runner.station.home)
+    assert "/home" in routes(fake)

+def test_per_poll_guard_ignores_a_frozen_wrist_when_the_scene_changes(fake, write_plan, tmp_path):
+    q = "Is a gripper holding a black ball?"
+    path = write_plan([stage("ball_1", timeout_s=1.5, on_fail="skip")], verifier="monitor",
+                      guard={"question": q, "stages": ["ball_"], "confirm": 2})
+    runner = build(fake, path, tmp_path, Script([mv(None)]))
+    n = {"i": 0}
+
+    def latest():
+        n["i"] += 1
+        return {"scene": b"scene%d" % n["i"], "wrist_r": b"frozen-wrist"}
+    runner.frames.latest = latest
+    runner.fast_guard = lambda f: (True, "white ball") if "wrist_r" in f else (False, "none")
+    result = runner.run()
+    assert not result.guard_tripped
+    assert len([e for e in events(runner.log, "guard_yes") if e.get("source") == "local"]) == 1
+

+def test_a_dead_camera_does_not_slow_the_live_one():
+    import time as _t
+    lf = LiveFrames("http://127.0.0.1:9", ("wrist_l", "wrist_r"))
+    lf._one = lambda role: (_t.sleep(1.0), None)[1] if role == "wrist_l" else (_t.sleep(0.01), b"jpeg")[1]
+    t0 = _t.monotonic()
+    got = [lf.grab(wait_s=0.05) for _ in range(5)]
+    took = _t.monotonic() - t0
+    assert all(g == {"wrist_r": b"jpeg"} for g in got)  # the live camera every tick
+    assert took < 0.5                                  # the dead one (1 s per try) never held a tick up

diff --git a/tools/cable_end/render_cable.py b/tools/cable_end/render_cable.py
new file mode 100644
index 0000000..11d4327
--- /dev/null
+++ b/tools/cable_end/render_cable.py
@@ -0,0 +1,466 @@
+"""Procedural model of the station's orange USB-A to USB-C cable, rendered with domain randomization.
+
+    blender -b -P render_cable.py -- OUT_DIR N_IMAGES BG_DIR [SEED]
+
+Model (mm, from the station photos IMG_2702-2704 and the USB specs):
+- USB-A plug: steel shell 12.0 x 4.5 x 12.0, two latch windows on the top face, dark tongue inside.
+- USB-C plug: steel shell 8.25 x 2.4 x 6.65, stadium cross-section, dark slot.
+- Ferrules: orange anodised aluminium, rounded boxes; A end 13.0 x 7.0 x 19.0, C end 11.0 x 6.0 x 18.0.
+- Strain reliefs: black, 15 mm, ribbed, tapering 6.0 -> 4.6 mm, orange showing between the ribs.
+- Cable: orange rubber, 4.0 mm, 150-220 mm long, random smooth bend in 3D.
+Each image: random cable shape and pose on a plane textured with a random crop of a real station frame or photo,
+random lights (sun + 1-3 area lights, colour temperature, strength), random camera (wide wrist-like or narrow
+lens, 5-40 cm away) aimed at one of: the USB-C end, the USB-A end, the cable middle, a strain relief, empty table.
+Random material roughness/colour jitter, depth of field and motion-free noise. Writes OUT_DIR/<label>/<n>.png
+(224 x 224) and OUT_DIR/labels.jsonl with the projected ferrule length in pixels.
+"""
+
+import json
+import math
+import os
+import random
+import sys
+
+import bpy
+from mathutils import Vector
+
+argv = sys.argv[sys.argv.index("--") + 1:]
+OUT, N, BG = os.path.abspath(argv[0]), int(argv[1]), os.path.abspath(argv[2])
+SEED = int(argv[3]) if len(argv) > 3 else 0
+rng = random.Random(SEED)
+MM = 0.001
+LABELS = ("usb_c", "usb_a", "none")
+
+BGS = [os.path.join(BG, f) for f in sorted(os.listdir(BG)) if f.lower().endswith((".jpg", ".png"))]
+
+
+# ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- scene setup
+def reset():
+    bpy.ops.wm.read_factory_settings(use_empty=True)
+    sc = bpy.context.scene
+    sc.render.engine = "CYCLES"
+    prefs = bpy.context.preferences.addons["cycles"].preferences
+    for kind in ("OPTIX", "CUDA"):
+        try:
+            prefs.compute_device_type = kind
+            prefs.get_devices()
+            for d in prefs.devices:
+                d.use = True
+            sc.cycles.device = "GPU"
+            break
+        except Exception:  # noqa: BLE001
+            continue
+    sc.cycles.samples = 48
+    sc.cycles.use_denoising = True
+    sc.render.resolution_x = sc.render.resolution_y = 224
+    sc.render.film_transparent = False
+    sc.view_settings.view_transform = "Standard"
+    sc.view_settings.exposure = rng.uniform(-1.8, 0.1)
+    return sc
+
+
+def mat(name, color, metallic=0.0, rough=0.5, spec=0.5):
+    m = bpy.data.materials.new(name)
+    m.use_nodes = True
+    b = m.node_tree.nodes["Principled BSDF"]
+    b.inputs["Base Color"].default_value = (*color, 1.0)
+    b.inputs["Metallic"].default_value = metallic
+    b.inputs["Roughness"].default_value = rough
+    return m
+
+
+def jitter(c, amt):
+    return tuple(min(1.0, max(0.0, v * (1 + rng.uniform(-amt, amt)))) for v in c)
+
+
+def mix(a, b, t):
+    return tuple(x + (y - x) * t for x, y in zip(a, b))
+
+
+BRIGHT = (0.9, 0.16, 0.005)      # linear RGB: saturated anodised orange
+SALMON = (0.80, 0.22, 0.10)      # linear RGB: the station cable's copper / salmon look on the wrist cameras
+
+
+def striped(name, base, rough):
+    """Black / orange braid bands along the object's local axis (cable and relief core)."""
+    m = mat(name, base, rough=rough)
+    nt = m.node_tree
+    wave = nt.nodes.new("ShaderNodeTexWave")
+    wave.wave_type = "BANDS"
+    wave.bands_direction = rng.choice(["X", "Y", "Z", "DIAGONAL"])
+    wave.inputs["Scale"].default_value = rng.uniform(40, 160)
+    wave.inputs["Distortion"].default_value = rng.uniform(0, 2)
+    coord = nt.nodes.new("ShaderNodeTexCoord")
+    nt.links.new(coord.outputs["Object"], wave.inputs["Vector"])
+    ramp = nt.nodes.new("ShaderNodeValToRGB")
+    ramp.color_ramp.elements[0].color = (0.02, 0.02, 0.02, 1)
+    ramp.color_ramp.elements[1].color = (*base, 1)
+    ramp.color_ramp.elements[0].position = rng.uniform(0.3, 0.5)
+    ramp.color_ramp.elements[1].position = ramp.color_ramp.elements[0].position + 0.05
+    nt.links.new(wave.outputs["Fac"], ramp.inputs["Fac"])
+    nt.links.new(ramp.outputs["Color"], nt.nodes["Principled BSDF"].inputs["Base Color"])
+    return m
+
+
+def materials():
+    orange = jitter(mix(BRIGHT, SALMON, rng.random()), 0.1)
+    rubber_rough = rng.uniform(0.4, 0.8)
+    rubber = striped("rubber", orange, rubber_rough) if rng.random() < 0.5 else mat("rubber", orange, rough=rubber_rough)
+    return {
+        "orange_anod": mat("anod", jitter(orange, 0.06), metallic=rng.uniform(0.2, 0.9),
+                           rough=rng.uniform(0.2, 0.55)),
+        "orange_rubber": rubber,
+        "black": mat("black", jitter((0.02, 0.02, 0.02), 0.5), rough=rng.uniform(0.4, 0.8)),
+        "steel": mat("steel", jitter((0.75, 0.75, 0.76), 0.1), metallic=1.0, rough=rng.uniform(0.1, 0.35)),
+        "dark": mat("dark", (0.03, 0.03, 0.035), rough=0.6),
+        "strip": mat("strip", jitter((0.03, 0.03, 0.03), 0.5), rough=rng.uniform(0.3, 0.7)),
+        "ball": mat("ball", jitter(mix((0.9, 0.25, 0.08), SALMON, rng.random()), 0.1), rough=rng.uniform(0.3, 0.7)),
+        "skin": mat("skin", jitter(rng.choice([(0.6, 0.35, 0.25), (0.35, 0.17, 0.1), (0.15, 0.07, 0.04),
+                                              (0.8, 0.5, 0.4)]), 0.15), rough=0.5),
+        "blue": mat("blue", jitter((0.02, 0.25, 0.6), 0.15), rough=0.5),
+    }
+
+
+def power_strip(M, face_at, side):
+    """Black power strip whose long side face (the USB ports) is at face_at, facing `side` (horizontal unit).
+    Returns (along unit vector, strip height)."""
+    L, W, H = rng.uniform(0.18, 0.3), rng.uniform(0.045, 0.06), rng.uniform(0.03, 0.045)
+    box = rbox("strip", (L, W, H), 0.004, M["strip"])
+    c = face_at - side * (W / 2)
+    box.location = (c.x + side.y * rng.uniform(-0.3, 0.3) * L, c.y - side.x * rng.uniform(-0.3, 0.3) * L, H / 2)
+    box.rotation_euler = (0, 0, math.atan2(side.x, -side.y))
+    bpy.context.view_layer.update()
+    return Vector((-side.y, side.x, 0)), H
+
+
+def yaw_for(d):
+    """Root yaw so the end's local +Y (towards the tip) points along horizontal d."""
+    return math.atan2(-d.x, d.y)
+
+
+def distractors(M, centre, n):
+    """Golf ball, finger stand-ins, blue block near the cable: the orange-ish things that are not ends."""
+    for _ in range(n):
+        p = centre + Vector((rng.uniform(-0.06, 0.06), rng.uniform(-0.06, 0.06), 0))
+        k = rng.random()
+        if k < 0.4:
+            r = 0.02135
+            bpy.ops.mesh.primitive_uv_sphere_add(radius=r, location=(p.x, p.y, r))
+            o = bpy.context.object
+            o.data.materials.append(M["ball"])
+            bpy.ops.object.shade_smooth()
+        elif k < 0.75:
+            r = rng.uniform(0.007, 0.01)
+            bpy.ops.mesh.primitive_cylinder_add(radius=r, depth=rng.uniform(0.04, 0.08),
+                                                location=(p.x, p.y, r + rng.uniform(0, 0.04)))
+            o = bpy.context.object
+            o.rotation_euler = (math.pi / 2 + rng.uniform(-0.6, 0.6), 0, rng.uniform(0, 6.28))
+            bev = o.modifiers.new("b", "BEVEL")
+            bev.width, bev.segments = r * 0.9, 6
+            o.data.materials.append(M["skin"])
+        else:
+            s = rng.uniform(0.025, 0.04)
+            o = rbox("block", (s, s, s), 0.002, M["blue"])
+            o.location = (p.x, p.y, s / 2)
+            o.rotation_euler = (0, 0, rng.uniform(0, 6.28))
+    bpy.context.view_layer.update()
+
+
+def rbox(name, size, bevel, m):
+    bpy.ops.mesh.primitive_cube_add(size=1)
+    o = bpy.context.object
+    o.name = name
+    o.scale = size
+    bpy.ops.object.transform_apply(scale=True)
+    if bevel > 0:
+        mod = o.modifiers.new("bev", "BEVEL")
+        mod.width = bevel
+        mod.segments = 4
+    o.data.materials.append(m)
+    return o
+
+
+def stadium(name, w, h, length, m):
+    """Rounded-end slab along +Y (the USB-C shell)."""
+    o = rbox(name, (w, length, h), h * 0.49, m)
+    return o
+
+
+def build_end(kind, M):
+    """One plug + ferrule + strain relief, along +Y from the origin (relief at y<0, tip at y>0). Returns
+    (parent empty, tip centre local, ferrule centre local, ferrule length)."""
+    parts = []
+    if kind == "a":
+        fw, fh, fl = 13.0, 7.0, 19.0
+        tw, th, tl = 12.0, 4.5, 12.0
+    else:
+        fw, fh, fl = 11.0, 6.0, 18.0
+        tw, th, tl = 8.25, 2.4, 6.65
+    fw, fh, fl = (v * rng.uniform(0.95, 1.05) for v in (fw, fh, fl))
+    fer = rbox("ferrule", (fw * MM, fl * MM, fh * MM), min(fw, fh) * 0.25 * MM, M["orange_anod"])
+    fer.location = (0, fl / 2 * MM, 0)
+    parts.append(fer)
+    if kind == "a":
+        shell = rbox("shell", (tw * MM, tl * MM, th * MM), 0.3 * MM, M["steel"])
+        shell.location = (0, (fl + tl / 2) * MM, 0)
+        tongue = rbox("tongue", ((tw - 1.2) * MM, (tl - 0.5) * MM, (th - 1.6) * MM), 0, M["dark"])
+        tongue.location = (0, (fl + tl / 2 + 0.3) * MM, -0.3 * MM)
+        parts += [shell, tongue]
+        for sx in (-2.5, 2.5):   # latch windows on the top face
+            win = rbox("win", (2.0 * MM, 2.5 * MM, 0.3 * MM), 0, M["dark"])
+            win.location = (sx * MM, (fl + tl * 0.55) * MM, (th / 2 + 0.05) * MM)
+            parts.append(win)
+    else:
+        shell = stadium("shell", tw * MM, th * MM, tl * MM, M["steel"])
+        shell.location = (0, (fl + tl / 2) * MM, 0)
+        slot = rbox("slot", ((tw - 1.4) * MM, 0.3 * MM, (th - 1.0) * MM), (th - 1.0) * 0.45 * MM, M["dark"])
+        slot.location = (0, (fl + tl + 0.05) * MM, 0)
+        parts += [shell, slot]
+    # strain relief: tapering ribs
+    n_rib = 7
+    for k in range(n_rib):
+        r = (3.0 - 0.7 * k / n_rib) * MM
+        bpy.ops.mesh.primitive_cylinder_add(radius=r, depth=1.3 * MM, vertices=24)
+        rib = bpy.context.object
+        rib.rotation_euler = (math.pi / 2, 0, 0)
+        rib.location = (0, -(1.0 + k * 2.1) * MM, 0)
+        rib.data.materials.append(M["black"])
+        parts.append(rib)
+    bpy.ops.mesh.primitive_cylinder_add(radius=2.2 * MM, depth=15 * MM, vertices=24)
+    core = bpy.context.object
+    core.rotation_euler = (math.pi / 2, 0, 0)
+    core.location = (0, -7.5 * MM, 0)
+    core.data.materials.append(M["orange_rubber"])
+    parts.append(core)
+    root = bpy.data.objects.new(f"end_{kind}", None)
+    bpy.context.collection.objects.link(root)
+    for p in parts:
+        p.parent = root
+    tip = Vector((0, (fl + tl * 0.5) * MM, 0))
+    return root, tip, Vector((0, fl / 2 * MM, 0)), fl * MM
+
+
+def cable_curve(p0, d0, p1, d1, M):
+    """Orange cable from the C relief end to the A relief end: a Bezier with tangents along each end's axis."""
+    cu = bpy.data.curves.new("cable", "CURVE")
+    cu.dimensions = "3D"
+    cu.bevel_depth = 2.0 * MM
+    cu.bevel_resolution = 4
+    sp = cu.splines.new("BEZIER")
+    sp.bezier_points.add(1)
+    k = (p1 - p0).length * rng.uniform(0.3, 0.6)
+    for bp, p, d, sign in ((sp.bezier_points[0], p0, d0, -1), (sp.bezier_points[1], p1, d1, -1)):
+        bp.co = p
+        bp.handle_left_type = bp.handle_right_type = "FREE"
+        bp.handle_right = p + d * sign * k * (1 if bp is sp.bezier_points[0] else -1)
+        bp.handle_left = p - (bp.handle_right - p)
+    o = bpy.data.objects.new("cable", cu)
+    bpy.context.collection.objects.link(o)
+    o.data.materials.append(M["orange_rubber"])
+    return o
+
+
+def place(root, pos, yaw, pitch=0.0, roll=0.0):
+    root.location = pos
+    root.rotation_euler = (pitch, roll, yaw)
+    bpy.context.view_layer.update()
+
+
+def world_pt(root, local):
+    return root.matrix_world @ local
+
+
+def table(sc):
+    bpy.ops.mesh.primitive_plane_add(size=1.2)
+    pl = bpy.context.object
+    m = bpy.data.materials.new("table")
+    m.use_nodes = True
+    nt = m.node_tree
+    bsdf = nt.nodes["Principled BSDF"]
+    bsdf.inputs["Roughness"].default_value = rng.uniform(0.3, 0.9)
+    if BGS and rng.random() < 0.85:
+        tex = nt.nodes.new("ShaderNodeTexImage")
+        tex.image = bpy.data.images.load(rng.choice(BGS))
+        mapn = nt.nodes.new("ShaderNodeMapping")
+        coord = nt.nodes.new("ShaderNodeTexCoord")
+        s = rng.uniform(0.6, 3.0)
+        mapn.inputs["Scale"].default_value = (s, s, 1)
+        mapn.inputs["Rotation"].default_value = (0, 0, rng.uniform(0, 6.28))
+        mapn.inputs["Location"].default_value = (rng.random(), rng.random(), 0)
+        nt.links.new(coord.outputs["UV"], mapn.inputs["Vector"])
+        nt.links.new(mapn.outputs["Vector"], tex.inputs["Vector"])
+        nt.links.new(tex.outputs["Color"], bsdf.inputs["Base Color"])
+    else:
+        g = rng.uniform(0.05, 0.9)
+        bsdf.inputs["Base Color"].default_value = (*jitter((g, g, g * rng.uniform(0.9, 1.05)), 0.1), 1)
+    pl.data.materials.append(m)
+    return pl
+
+
+def kelvin(t):
+    t = t / 100.0
+    r = 1.0 if t <= 66 else min(1.0, 1.29 * (t - 60) ** -0.133)
+    g = min(1.0, (0.39 * math.log(t) - 0.63)) if t <= 66 else min(1.0, 1.13 * (t - 60) ** -0.0755)
+    b = 1.0 if t >= 66 else (0.0 if t <= 19 else min(1.0, 0.543 * math.log(t - 10) - 1.19))
+    return (max(r, 0), max(g, 0), max(b, 0))
+
+
+def lights(sc):
+    w = bpy.data.worlds.new("w")
+    sc.world = w
+    w.use_nodes = True
+    w.node_tree.nodes["Background"].inputs["Color"].default_value = (*kelvin(rng.uniform(3000, 7500)), 1)
+    w.node_tree.nodes["Background"].inputs["Strength"].default_value = rng.uniform(0.05, 0.6)
+    bpy.ops.object.light_add(type="SUN", rotation=(rng.uniform(0, 1.2), rng.uniform(-0.6, 0.6), rng.uniform(0, 6.28)))
+    sun = bpy.context.object
+    sun.data.energy = rng.uniform(0.0, 2.5)
+    sun.data.color = kelvin(rng.uniform(3500, 7000))
+    for _ in range(rng.randint(1, 3)):
+        bpy.ops.object.light_add(type="AREA", location=(rng.uniform(-0.6, 0.6), rng.uniform(-0.6, 0.6),
+                                                         rng.uniform(0.4, 2.5)))
+        a = bpy.context.object
+        a.data.size = rng.uniform(0.2, 1.5)
+        a.data.energy = rng.uniform(5, 70)
+        a.data.color = kelvin(rng.uniform(3000, 7500))
+        direction = Vector((0, 0, 0)) - a.location
+        a.rotation_euler = direction.to_track_quat("-Z", "Y").to_euler()
+
+
+def camera(sc, target, ferrule_len, kind):
+    cam_data = bpy.data.cameras.new("cam")
+    wide = rng.random() < 0.6
+    cam_data.lens = rng.uniform(10, 16) if wide else rng.uniform(24, 60)
+    cam_data.clip_start = 0.001   # the camera is 2-30 cm from the plug; the 10 cm default clips it away
+    cam = bpy.data.objects.new("cam", cam_data)
+    bpy.context.collection.objects.link(cam)
+    sc.camera = cam
+    # distance so the ferrule spans a random share of the frame (what the classifier crop will look like)
+    frac = rng.uniform(0.18, 0.55)
+    fov = 2 * math.atan(cam_data.sensor_width / 2 / cam_data.lens)
+    dist = ferrule_len / frac / (2 * math.tan(fov / 2))
+    el = rng.uniform(math.radians(20), math.radians(88))
+    az = rng.uniform(0, 2 * math.pi)
+    off = Vector((math.cos(az) * math.cos(el), math.sin(az) * math.cos(el), math.sin(el))) * dist
+    cam.location = target + off
+    q = (target - cam.location).to_track_quat("-Z", "Y")
+    cam.rotation_euler = q.to_euler()
+    cam.rotation_euler.rotate_axis("Z", rng.uniform(-math.pi, math.pi))
+    if rng.random() < 0.3:
+        cam_data.dof.use_dof = True
+        cam_data.dof.focus_distance = dist * rng.uniform(0.85, 1.15)
+        cam_data.dof.aperture_fstop = rng.uniform(2.0, 8.0)
+    return cam, frac
+
+
+def seen(sc, cam, tgt, root):
+    """Line of sight from the camera to the target: for an end, the first thing hit must be part of that end (or
+    nothing before it); for 'none', the camera must not be inside or right against an object."""
+    dg = bpy.context.evaluated_depsgraph_get()
+    o = cam.matrix_world.translation
+    if sc.ray_cast(dg, o, Vector((0, 0, 1)), distance=1.0)[0]:
+        return False     # something right above the camera: it is inside an object (the strip, the ball)
+    v = tgt - o
+    dist = v.length
+    hit, loc, _, _, obj, _ = sc.ray_cast(dg, o, v.normalized(), distance=dist * 1.2)
+    if not hit:
+        return root is None
+    if root is None:
+        return (loc - o).length > 0.4 * dist
+    return obj.parent == root or (loc - o).length > dist * 0.97
+
+
+def one(i):
+    sc = reset()
+    M = materials()
+    table(sc)
+    lights(sc)
+    c_root, c_tip, c_fer, c_fl = build_end("c", M)
+    a_root, a_tip, a_fer, a_fl = build_end("a", M)
+    length = rng.uniform(0.15, 0.22)
+    yaw = rng.uniform(0, 2 * math.pi)
+    lift = 3.5 * MM
+    place(c_root, Vector((0, 0, lift)), yaw + rng.uniform(-0.9, 0.9), rng.uniform(-0.15, 0.15),
+          rng.choice([0, math.pi / 2, math.pi, -math.pi / 2]) + rng.uniform(-0.2, 0.2))
+    p_a = Vector((math.cos(yaw) * length * 0.7 + rng.uniform(-0.05, 0.05),
+                  math.sin(yaw) * length * 0.7 + rng.uniform(-0.05, 0.05), lift))
+    place(a_root, p_a, yaw + math.pi + rng.uniform(-1.2, 1.2), rng.uniform(-0.15, 0.15),
+          rng.choice([0, math.pi / 2, math.pi, -math.pi / 2]) + rng.uniform(-0.2, 0.2))
+    if rng.random() < 0.15:      # held in the air: tilt the whole end up off the table
+        c_root.location.z += rng.uniform(0.01, 0.08)
+        c_root.rotation_euler.x += rng.uniform(-0.8, 0.8)
+    bpy.context.view_layer.update()
+    rel = lambda root: (root.matrix_world @ Vector((0, -15 * MM, 0)),
+                        (root.matrix_world.to_3x3() @ Vector((0, -1, 0))).normalized())
+    label = rng.choices(LABELS, weights=(0.4, 0.3, 0.3))[0]
+    seated = rng.random() < (0.5 if label == "usb_a" else 0.25)
+    if seated:
+        # the A end seated in a power strip's USB port, often with other cables' A ends next to it
+        side = Vector((-p_a.x, -p_a.y, 0)).normalized()
+        along, H = power_strip(M, p_a, side)
+        z = H * rng.uniform(0.35, 0.65)
+        gap = rng.uniform(0, 1.5) * MM
+        a_root.location = p_a + side * (a_fl + gap) + Vector((0, 0, z))
+        a_root.rotation_euler = (0, rng.choice([0, math.pi]), yaw_for(-side))
+        for k in rng.sample([-2, -1, 1, 2], rng.randint(0, 3)):
+            n_root, _, _, n_fl = build_end("a", M)
+            n_root.location = (p_a + along * k * rng.uniform(0.022, 0.035) + side * (n_fl + rng.uniform(0, 1.5) * MM)
+                               + Vector((0, 0, z)))
+            n_root.rotation_euler = (0, rng.choice([0, math.pi]), yaw_for(-side))
+            bpy.context.view_layer.update()
+            pn, dn = rel(n_root)
+            far = pn + side * rng.uniform(0.08, 0.15) + along * rng.uniform(-0.05, 0.05)
+            far.z = 2 * MM
+            cable_curve(pn, dn, far, -side, M)
+        bpy.context.view_layer.update()
+    pc, dc = rel(c_root)
+    pa, da = rel(a_root)
+    cable_curve(pc, dc, pa, da, M)
+    spots = []
+    if rng.random() < (0.7 if label == "none" else 0.35):
+        centre = rng.choice([pc, pa, (pc + pa) / 2])
+        centre = Vector((centre.x, centre.y, 0))
+        before = set(bpy.data.objects)
+        distractors(M, centre, rng.randint(1, 3))
+        spots = [o.location.copy() for o in bpy.data.objects if o not in before]
+    if label == "usb_c":
+        tgt = (world_pt(c_root, c_fer) + world_pt(c_root, c_tip)) / 2
+        fl = c_fl
+    elif label == "usb_a":
+        tgt = world_pt(a_root, a_fer) if seated else (world_pt(a_root, a_fer) + world_pt(a_root, a_tip)) / 2
+        fl = a_fl
+    else:
+        what = rng.random()
+        if spots and rng.random() < 0.5:
+            tgt = rng.choice(spots).copy()
+        elif what < 0.4:
+            tgt = (pc + pa) / 2 + Vector((rng.uniform(-0.03, 0.03), rng.uniform(-0.03, 0.03), 0))
+        elif what < 0.7:
+            tgt = rng.choice([pc, pa]) + Vector((0, 0, 0))
+        else:
+            tgt = Vector((rng.uniform(-0.3, 0.3), rng.uniform(-0.3, 0.3), 0))
+        fl = rng.uniform(15, 19) * MM
+    tgt += Vector((rng.uniform(-1, 1), rng.uniform(-1, 1), 0)) * fl * 0.25   # not always dead centre
+    want = {"usb_c": c_root, "usb_a": a_root}.get(label)
+    for _ in range(15):
+        cam, frac = camera(sc, tgt, fl, label)
+        if seen(sc, cam, tgt, want):
+            break
+        bpy.data.objects.remove(cam, do_unlink=True)
+    else:
+        return None      # no clear view of the end in this scene: skip it rather than mislabel it
+    d = os.path.join(OUT, label)
+    os.makedirs(d, exist_ok=True)
+    path = os.path.join(d, f"{SEED:03d}_{i:05d}.png")
+    sc.render.filepath = path
+    bpy.ops.render.render(write_still=True)
+    return {"file": os.path.relpath(path, OUT), "label": label, "ferrule_frac": round(frac, 3), "seed": SEED, "i": i}
+
+
+os.makedirs(OUT, exist_ok=True)
+with open(os.path.join(OUT, f"labels_{SEED:03d}.jsonl"), "a", encoding="utf-8") as f:
+    for i in range(N):
+        rec = one(i)
+        if rec is None:
+            continue
+        f.write(json.dumps(rec) + "\n")
+        f.flush()
diff --git a/tools/cable_end/train.py b/tools/cable_end/train.py
new file mode 100644

--- /dev/null
+++ b/tools/cable_end/train.py
@@ -0,0 +1,241 @@
+"""Train the cable-end classifier (usb_c / usb_a / none) on Blender renders + real station crops; export ONNX.
+
+    python train.py [--epochs 12] [--real-train 0.5] [--out cable_end.onnx]
+
+Inputs: synth/<label>/*.png (render_cable.py), real/cands/*.png + real/labels.json + real/cands.json.
+Real crops are split by source video (crops of one video never sit on both sides); the high-resolution photos
+(table_photos/*) are always test. 'unsure' real crops are never used. Reports per-class precision/recall on the real
+test crops for a synthetic-only model and for synthetic + real-train, and checks the exported ONNX against torch
+with OpenCV's DNN module (what the harness runs).
+"""
+
+from __future__ import annotations
+
+import argparse
+import glob
+import json
+import os
+import random
+
+import cv2
+import numpy as np
+import torch
+import torch.nn as nn
+import torchvision
+
+LABELS = ["usb_c", "usb_a", "none"]
+SIZE = 96
+MEAN = np.array([0.485, 0.456, 0.406], np.float32)
+STD = np.array([0.229, 0.224, 0.225], np.float32)
+HERE = os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__))
+
+
+def prep(bgr: np.ndarray) -> np.ndarray:
+    """BGR uint8 (any size) -> CHW float32 normalised, SIZE x SIZE. The harness does the same in numpy."""
+    im = cv2.resize(bgr, (SIZE, SIZE), interpolation=cv2.INTER_AREA)[:, :, ::-1].astype(np.float32) / 255.0
+    return ((im - MEAN) / STD).transpose(2, 0, 1).copy()
+
+
+def augment(bgr: np.ndarray, rng: random.Random, synth: bool) -> np.ndarray:
+    h, w = bgr.shape[:2]
+    # zoom/shift/rotate: the runtime crop is centred on a detected ferrule but not perfectly
+    z = rng.uniform(0.75, 1.35) if synth else rng.uniform(0.85, 1.15)
+    ang = rng.uniform(-180, 180)
+    M = cv2.getRotationMatrix2D((w / 2 + rng.uniform(-0.1, 0.1) * w, h / 2 + rng.uniform(-0.1, 0.1) * h), ang, z)
+    bgr = cv2.warpAffine(bgr, M, (w, h), borderMode=cv2.BORDER_REFLECT)
+    if rng.random() < 0.5:
+        bgr = cv2.flip(bgr, 1)
+    hsv = cv2.cvtColor(bgr, cv2.COLOR_BGR2HSV).astype(np.float32)
+    hsv[..., 0] = (hsv[..., 0] + rng.uniform(-4, 4)) % 180
+    hsv[..., 1] *= rng.uniform(0.5, 1.3)
+    hsv[..., 2] *= rng.uniform(0.6, 1.4)
+    bgr = cv2.cvtColor(np.clip(hsv, 0, 255).astype(np.uint8), cv2.COLOR_HSV2BGR)
+    # the wrist cameras: small, soft, compressed. Shrink to a random low res and back, blur, JPEG.
+    if synth or rng.random() < 0.3:
+        lo = rng.randint(24, 96)
+        bgr = cv2.resize(cv2.resize(bgr, (lo, lo), interpolation=cv2.INTER_AREA), (w, h))
+    if rng.random() < 0.4:
+        k = rng.choice([3, 5])
+        bgr = cv2.GaussianBlur(bgr, (k, k), 0)
+    if rng.random() < 0.3:   # motion blur
+        k = rng.randint(3, 9)
+        ker = np.zeros((k, k), np.float32)
+        ker[k // 2, :] = 1.0 / k
+        ker = cv2.warpAffine(ker, cv2.getRotationMatrix2D((k / 2, k / 2), rng.uniform(0, 180), 1), (k, k))
+        bgr = cv2.filter2D(bgr, -1, ker / max(ker.sum(), 1e-6))
+    if rng.random() < 0.6:
+        ok, enc = cv2.imencode(".jpg", bgr, [cv2.IMWRITE_JPEG_QUALITY, rng.randint(30, 90)])
+        bgr = cv2.imdecode(enc, cv2.IMREAD_COLOR)
+    if rng.random() < 0.3:
+        bgr = np.clip(bgr.astype(np.float32) + np.random.normal(0, rng.uniform(2, 10), bgr.shape), 0, 255).astype(np.uint8)
+    return bgr
+
+
+class DS(torch.utils.data.Dataset):
+    def __init__(self, items, train: bool, seed=0):
+        self.items, self.train = items, train
+        self.rng = random.Random(seed)
+        self.cache: dict[str, np.ndarray] = {}
+
+    def __len__(self):
+        return len(self.items)
+
+    def __getitem__(self, i):
+        path, y, synth = self.items[i]
+        im = self.cache.get(path)
+        if im is None:
+            im = cv2.imread(path)
+            if synth:
+                im = cv2.resize(im, (128, 128), interpolation=cv2.INTER_AREA)
+            self.cache[path] = im
+        if self.train:
+            im = augment(im, self.rng, synth)
+        return torch.from_numpy(prep(im)), y
+
+
+def real_items():
+    labels = {int(k): v for k, v in json.load(open(os.path.join(HERE, "real/labels.json"))).items()}
+    cands = {c["id"]: c for c in json.load(open(os.path.join(HERE, "real/cands.json")))}
+    out = []
+    for i, lab in labels.items():
+        if lab not in LABELS:
+            continue
+        out.append((os.path.join(HERE, f"real/cands/{i:05d}.png"), LABELS.index(lab), cands[i]["src"]))
+    return out
+
+
+def synth_items():
+    out = []
+    for y, lab in enumerate(LABELS):
+        for p in glob.glob(os.path.join(HERE, "synth", lab, "*.png")):
+            out.append((p, y, True))
+    return out
+
+
+def model():
+    m = torchvision.models.mobilenet_v3_small(weights=torchvision.models.MobileNet_V3_Small_Weights.DEFAULT)
+    m.classifier[3] = nn.Linear(m.classifier[3].in_features, len(LABELS))
+    return m
+
+
+def train(items, epochs, dev, seed=0):
+    torch.manual_seed(seed)
+    m = model().to(dev)
+    counts = np.bincount([y for _, y, _ in items], minlength=len(LABELS)).astype(np.float32)
+    w = torch.tensor(counts.sum() / np.maximum(counts, 1) / len(LABELS), device=dev)
+    dl = torch.utils.data.DataLoader(DS(items, True, seed), batch_size=128, shuffle=True, num_workers=0)
+    opt = torch.optim.AdamW(m.parameters(), lr=1e-3, weight_decay=1e-4)
+    sched = torch.optim.lr_scheduler.OneCycleLR(opt, 1e-3, total_steps=epochs * len(dl))
+    lossf = nn.CrossEntropyLoss(weight=w, label_smoothing=0.05)
+    for ep in range(epochs):
+        m.train()
+        tot = n = 0
+        for x, y in dl:
+            x, y = x.to(dev), y.to(dev)
+            loss = lossf(m(x), y)
+            opt.zero_grad()
+            loss.backward()
+            opt.step()
+            sched.step()
+            tot += loss.item() * len(y)
+            n += len(y)
+        print(f"  epoch {ep + 1}/{epochs} loss {tot / n:.3f}", flush=True)
+    return m.eval()
+
+
+@torch.no_grad()
+def predict(m, items, dev):
+    ds = DS(items, False)
+    xs = torch.stack([ds[i][0] for i in range(len(ds))])
+    out = []
+    for k in range(0, len(xs), 256):
+        out.append(torch.softmax(m(xs[k:k + 256].to(dev)), 1).cpu().numpy())
+    return np.concatenate(out) if out else np.zeros((0, len(LABELS)))
+
+
+def report(name, probs, ys):
+    pred = probs.argmax(1)
+    print(f"{name}: n={len(ys)} accuracy {np.mean(pred == ys):.3f}")
+    cm = np.zeros((3, 3), int)
+    for t, p in zip(ys, pred):
+        cm[t, p] += 1
+    print("   true\\pred " + " ".join(f"{l:>6}" for l in LABELS))
+    for t in range(3):
+        print(f"   {LABELS[t]:>9} " + " ".join(f"{cm[t, p]:>6}" for p in range(3)))
+    for k, l in enumerate(LABELS):
+        tp = cm[k, k]
+        prec = tp / max(cm[:, k].sum(), 1)
+        rec = tp / max(cm[k].sum(), 1)
+        print(f"   {l}: precision {prec:.2f} recall {rec:.2f} (n={cm[k].sum()})")
+    return cm
+
+
+def at_threshold(probs, ys, t):
+    """What the harness does: report an end only when its class prob >= t (else nothing)."""
+    pred = probs.argmax(1)
+    conf = probs.max(1)
+    for k, l in enumerate(LABELS[:2]):
+        said = (pred == k) & (conf >= t)
+        tp = int((said & (ys == k)).sum())
+        print(f"   at p>={t}: {l} precision {tp / max(int(said.sum()), 1):.2f} recall {tp / max(int((ys == k).sum()), 1):.2f}"
+              f" ({int(said.sum())} reported)")
+
+
+def main():
+    ap = argparse.ArgumentParser()
+    ap.add_argument("--epochs", type=int, default=12)
+    ap.add_argument("--real-train", type=float, default=0.5, help="share of real source videos used for training")
+    ap.add_argument("--out", default=os.path.join(HERE, "cable_end.onnx"))
+    ap.add_argument("--final", choices=["real", "synth+real"], default="real", help="data for the shipped model")
+    ap.add_argument("--final-only", action="store_true", help="skip the held-out comparisons")
+    args = ap.parse_args()
+    dev = "cuda" if torch.cuda.is_available() else "cpu"
+    print("device", dev)
+    synth = synth_items()
+    real = real_items()
+    print("synthetic", np.bincount([y for _, y, _ in synth], minlength=3), "real", np.bincount([y for _, y, _ in real], minlength=3))
+    srcs = sorted({s for _, _, s in real if "table_photos" not in s})
+    rng = random.Random(0)
+    rng.shuffle(srcs)
+    train_src = set(srcs[: int(len(srcs) * args.real_train)])
+    real_train = [(p, y, False) for p, y, s in real if s in train_src]
+    real_test = [(p, y, False) for p, y, s in real if s not in train_src]
+    ys_test = np.array([y for _, y, _ in real_test])
+    photo = [(p, y, False) for p, y, s in real if "table_photos" in s]
+    ys_photo = np.array([y for _, y, _ in photo])
+    print(f"real train {len(real_train)} test {len(real_test)} (of which photos {len(photo)})")
+
+    def show(name, m):
+        p = predict(m, real_test, dev)
+        report(f"{name} on held-out real videos", p, ys_test)
+        at_threshold(p, ys_test, 0.7)
+        report(f"{name} on held-out photos", predict(m, photo, dev), ys_photo)
+
+    if not args.final_only:
+        print("== synthetic only")
+        show("synthetic-only", train(synth, args.epochs, dev))
+        print("== real only (real oversampled x8)")
+        show("real-only", train(real_train * 8, args.epochs, dev))
+        print("== synthetic + real (real oversampled x8)")
+        show("synthetic+real", train(synth + real_train * 8, args.epochs, dev))
+
+    # The shipped model: every labelled crop (all videos and the photos). 7 Nov, 15 epochs, held-out videos:
+    # real-only 0.944 accuracy vs synthetic+real 0.918 (photos 0.73 vs 0.56), so the renders stay out by default.
+    everything = [(p, y, False) for p, y, s in real]
+    print(f"== final ({args.final}): all {len(everything)} real crops")
+    m1 = train((synth if args.final == "synth+real" else []) + everything * 8, args.epochs, dev)
+    m1 = m1.cpu().eval()
+    torch.onnx.export(m1, torch.zeros(1, 3, SIZE, SIZE), args.out, input_names=["x"], output_names=["logits"],
+                      opset_version=17, dynamo=False)
+    net = cv2.dnn.readNetFromONNX(args.out)
+    xs = np.stack([DS(real_test, False)[i][0].numpy() for i in range(min(64, len(real_test)))])
+    net.setInput(xs)
+    o_cv = net.forward()
+    with torch.no_grad():
+        o_t = m1(torch.from_numpy(xs)).numpy()
+    print(f"ONNX via OpenCV vs torch: max |diff| {np.abs(o_cv - o_t).max():.2e}, argmax agree {np.mean(o_cv.argmax(1) == o_t.argmax(1)):.3f}")
+    print("wrote", args.out, f"{os.path.getsize(args.out) / 1e6:.1f} MB")
+
+
+if __name__ == "__main__":
+    main()
diff --git a/tools/fit_stage_curves.py b/tools/fit_stage_curves.py
new file mode 100644

+++ b/tools/fit_stage_curves.py

+"""Fit each stage's success-vs-time curve from run logs, for harness.decide's planner.
+
+    python tools/fit_stage_curves.py RUNS_DIR [RUNS_DIR ...] [--out plans/stage_curves.json] [--horizon 295]
+
+Reads every segments.jsonl under the given directories. One episode = one stage in one run: the time from its
+first attempt's start to the end of its first successful attempt (recoveries and retries included), or, if it
+never succeeded, the total time spent on it (censored: we only know it took longer than that).
+
+Model (harness.decide.StageModel): F(tau) = p_max * (1 - exp(-(tau - t_min) / tau_c)) for tau > t_min. Fitted by
+maximum likelihood on a grid, with censoring: a success at tau adds log f(tau), a censored episode at c adds
+log(1 - F(c)). Stage names are grouped by stripping trailing digits and underscores (ball_1..ball_8 -> ball).
+
+Caveat: most logs so far are from Dream's MuJoCo YAM sim, not the Metal station. Fit station logs separately
+before trusting the timeouts for a match.
+"""
+
+from __future__ import annotations
+
+import argparse
+import json
+import math
+import sys
+from collections import defaultdict
+from pathlib import Path
+
+sys.path.insert(0, str(Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]))
+from harness.decide import StageModel, plan_timeouts  # noqa: E402
+
+GRID_P = [i / 50 for i in range(1, 51)]
+GRID_TAU = [2.0 * 1.12 ** k for k in range(45)]          # 2 s .. ~330 s
+
+
+def episodes(dirs: list[Path]) -> dict[str, list[tuple[float, bool]]]:
+    """stage group -> [(time, succeeded)]"""
+    out: dict[str, list[tuple[float, bool]]] = defaultdict(list)
+    for d in dirs:
+        for seg_file in d.rglob("segments.jsonl"):
+            runs: dict[str, list[dict]] = defaultdict(list)
+            for line in seg_file.read_text(encoding="utf-8", errors="replace").splitlines():
+                try:
+                    r = json.loads(line)
+                except json.JSONDecodeError:
+                    continue
+                if r.get("t_start") is None or r.get("t_end") is None:
+                    continue
+                runs[r["stage"]].append(r)
+            for name, segs in runs.items():
+                segs.sort(key=lambda r: r["t_start"])
+                t0 = segs[0]["t_start"]
+                ok = next((r for r in segs if r.get("kind") == "stage" and r.get("outcome") == "ok"), None)
+                if ok is not None:
+                    out[group(name)].append((ok["t_end"] - t0, True))
+                else:
+                    out[group(name)].append((max(r["t_end"] for r in segs) - t0, False))
+    return out
+
+
+def group(name: str) -> str:
+    return name.rstrip("0123456789").rstrip("_") or name
+
+
+def loglik(eps: list[tuple[float, bool]], p: float, tau: float, tmin: float) -> float:
+    ll = 0.0
+    for t, ok in eps:
+        if ok:
+            if t <= tmin:
+                return -math.inf
+            ll += math.log(p) - (t - tmin) / tau - math.log(tau)
+        else:
+            F = 0.0 if t <= tmin else p * (1 - math.exp(-(t - tmin) / tau))
+            ll += math.log(max(1e-12, 1 - F))
+    return ll
+
+
+def fit(eps: list[tuple[float, bool]]) -> tuple[float, float, float, float]:
+    """(p_max, tau_c, t_min, loglik)"""
+    succ = [t for t, ok in eps if ok]
+    tmins = [0.0] if not succ else sorted({0.0, *(round(min(succ) * f, 2) for f in (0.25, 0.5, 0.75, 0.95))})
+    best = (0.0, GRID_TAU[0], 0.0, -math.inf)
+    for tmin in tmins:
+        for p in GRID_P:
+            for tau in GRID_TAU:
+                ll = loglik(eps, p, tau, tmin)
+                if ll > best[3]:
+                    best = (p, tau, tmin, ll)
+    return best
+
+
+def main(argv: list[str] | None = None) -> int:
+    ap = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__.splitlines()[0])
+    ap.add_argument("dirs", nargs="+", type=Path)
+    ap.add_argument("--out", type=Path, default=None)
+    ap.add_argument("--min-episodes", type=int, default=5)
+    a = ap.parse_args(argv)
+    eps = episodes(a.dirs)
+    fitted = {}
+    print(f"{'stage':<24}{'n':>5}{'ok':>5}{'p_max':>8}{'tau_c s':>9}{'t_min s':>9}  median ok s")
+    for name, e in sorted(eps.items()):
+        if len(e) < a.min_episodes:
+            continue
+        p, tau, tmin, _ = fit(e)
+        succ = sorted(t for t, ok in e if ok)
+        med = f"{succ[len(succ) // 2]:.1f}" if succ else "-"
+        print(f"{name:<24}{len(e):>5}{len(succ):>5}{p:>8.2f}{tau:>9.1f}{tmin:>9.1f}  {med}")
+        fitted[name] = {"p_max": p, "tau_c": round(tau, 2), "t_min": tmin, "episodes": len(e), "successes": len(succ)}
+    if a.out:
+        a.out.write_text(json.dumps(fitted, indent=1) + "\n", encoding="utf-8")
+        print("wrote", a.out)
+    return 0
+
+
+def models(curves: dict, spec: list[tuple[str, str, float, bool]]) -> list[StageModel]:
+    """[(stage name, curve group, points, gate)] -> StageModels for plan_timeouts."""
+    out = []
+    for name, grp, value, gate in spec:
+        c = curves[grp]
+        out.append(StageModel(name, value, c["p_max"], c["tau_c"], c["t_min"], gate))
+    return out
+
+
+if __name__ == "__main__":
+    raise SystemExit(main())
diff --git a/tools/label_rollout.py b/tools/label_rollout.py
new file mode 100644
index 0000000..fa3163c
--- /dev/null
+++ b/tools/label_rollout.py
@@ -0,0 +1,97 @@
+"""Label Dream's own record of a harness run for fine-tuning.
+
+Dream keeps each run's record (the station's archiver copies it to ~/rollouts/zeroshot_<time>/): last-run.csv (20 Hz
+joints and commands) and last-run_inputs/inputs.jsonl (every model input: frames, state and the prompt it was given).
+The harness knows what those seconds were for. This joins the two:
+
+    python tools/label_rollout.py runs/<stamp> <rollout dir or its inputs.jsonl>
+
+and writes <rollout>/harness_labels.jsonl, one line per model input, plus harness_segments.json:
+  stage, attempt     the harness stage and attempt the input belongs to
+  prompt_sent        what Dream was told (stage prompt, a monitor rewrite, a recovery, an operator prompt)
+  source             stage | rewrite | recovery | operator | tower
+  label              the clean task label for training: the stage's own prompt (rewrites map back to it)
+  outcome            ok | failed for the attempt the input is in; usable = ok and not a recovery
+
+The clocks differ (Dream's t_s is monotonic, the harness logs epoch time). They are aligned on the first /start:
+its harness time is the first model input's time, to about a second.
+"""
+from __future__ import annotations
+
+import json
+import pathlib
+import sys
+
+
+def _load(path: pathlib.Path) -> list[dict]:
+    return [json.loads(line) for line in path.read_text().splitlines() if line.strip()]
+
+
+def _source(why: str) -> str:
+    why = why or ""
+    if why.startswith("monitor next_prompt"):
+        return "rewrite"
+    if why.startswith("recovery"):
+        return "recovery"
+    if why == "operator":
+        return "operator"
+    return "stage"
+
+
+def label(run: pathlib.Path, rollout: pathlib.Path) -> tuple[list[dict], list[dict]]:
+    inputs_path = rollout if rollout.suffix == ".jsonl" else rollout / "last-run_inputs" / "inputs.jsonl"
+    inputs = [r for r in _load(inputs_path) if r.get("kind") == "inputs"]
+    events = _load(run / "events.jsonl")
+    start = next(e for e in events if e["event"] == "start")
+    offset = start["t"] - inputs[0]["t_s"]   # harness epoch = Dream t_s + offset
+
+    # Spans: [t, stage, attempt, prompt, source, label], each running until the next prompt change.
+    spans, stage, attempt, base = [], None, 1, ""
+    outcomes: dict[tuple[str, int], str] = {}
+    for e in events:
+        ev = e["event"]
+        if ev == "stage_start":
+            stage, attempt, base = e["stage"], 1, e.get("prompt") or ""
+        elif ev == "tower_prompt" and e.get("next"):
+            base = e.get("prompt") or base
+        elif ev == "attempt_end":   # `retry` is logged after the retry's prompt, so count from here
+            outcomes[(e["stage"], e["attempt"])] = "ok" if e.get("ok") else "failed"
+            attempt = e["attempt"] + 1
+        elif ev == "prompt_change" and stage is not None:
+            src = _source(e.get("why"))
+            spans.append({"t": e["t"], "stage": stage, "attempt": attempt, "prompt_sent": e.get("prompt") or "",
+                          "source": src, "label": base if src in ("stage", "rewrite") else e.get("prompt") or ""})
+    rows = []
+    for r in inputs:
+        t = r["t_s"] + offset
+        span = next((s for s in reversed(spans) if s["t"] <= t + 0.5), None)
+        if span is None:
+            continue
+        outcome = outcomes.get((span["stage"], span["attempt"]), "unknown")
+        rows.append({"t_s": r["t_s"], **{k: span[k] for k in ("stage", "attempt", "prompt_sent", "source", "label")},
+                     "prompt_seen": (r.get("state") or {}).get("prompt"), "outcome": outcome,
+                     "usable": outcome == "ok" and span["source"] != "recovery"})
+    segs: list[dict] = []
+    for row in rows:
+        key = (row["stage"], row["attempt"])
+        if segs and (segs[-1]["stage"], segs[-1]["attempt"]) == key:
+            segs[-1]["t_end"] = row["t_s"]
+            segs[-1]["inputs"] += 1
+        else:
+            segs.append({"stage": row["stage"], "attempt": row["attempt"], "t_start": row["t_s"], "t_end": row["t_s"],
+                         "inputs": 1, "label": row["label"], "outcome": row["outcome"]})
+    return rows, segs
+
+
+if __name__ == "__main__":
+    run, rollout = pathlib.Path(sys.argv[1]), pathlib.Path(sys.argv[2])
+    rows, segs = label(run, rollout)
+    out = rollout.parent if rollout.suffix == ".jsonl" else rollout
+    (out / "harness_labels.jsonl").write_text("".join(json.dumps(r) + "\n" for r in rows))
+    (out / "harness_segments.json").write_text(json.dumps(segs, indent=2))
+    mismatch = sum(1 for r in rows if r["prompt_seen"] and r["prompt_seen"] != r["prompt_sent"])
+    for s in segs:
+        print(f"{s['outcome']:<7} {s['stage']:<18} a{s['attempt']}  {s['t_end'] - s['t_start']:5.0f} s  "
+              f"{s['inputs']:3d} inputs  {s['label'][:70]}")
+    print(f"{len(rows)} inputs labelled, {sum(r['usable'] for r in rows)} usable; prompt mismatches "
+          f"(alignment check, want ~0): {mismatch}  -> {out}/harness_labels.jsonl")
diff --git a/tools/start_poses.py b/tools/start_poses.py
new file mode 100644
index 0000000..9913da2
--- /dev/null
+++ b/tools/start_poses.py
@@ -0,0 +1,56 @@
+"""Build the start-pose reference for harness.start_pose from LeRobot teleop datasets.
+
+    python tools/start_poses.py OUT.json DATASET_DIR [DATASET_DIR ...]
+
+For every episode, the first frame of each task segment (a change of task_index starts a segment) becomes one
+reference start under that task's exact text. observation.state is [left 6 joints, left gripper, right 6 joints,
+right gripper] (the Metal/YAM follower layout in meta/info.json); grippers are dropped. Needs pyarrow.
+"""
+
+from __future__ import annotations
+
+import json
+import sys
+from pathlib import Path
+
+
+def segment_starts(dataset: Path) -> list[tuple[str, dict]]:
+    import pyarrow.parquet as pq  # noqa: PLC0415 - only this tool needs it
+
+    info = json.loads((dataset / "meta" / "info.json").read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
+    names = info["features"]["observation.state"]["names"]
+    idx = {side: [names.index(f"{side}_joint_{j}") for j in range(6)] for side in ("left", "right")}
+    tasks = {}
+    for line in (dataset / "meta" / "tasks.jsonl").read_text(encoding="utf-8").splitlines():
+        if line.strip():
+            t = json.loads(line)
+            tasks[t["task_index"]] = t["task"]
+    out = []
+    for f in sorted((dataset / "data").glob("chunk-*/episode_*.parquet")):
+        cols = pq.read_table(f, columns=["observation.state", "task_index"]).to_pydict()
+        prev, seg = None, 0
+        for state, ti in zip(cols["observation.state"], cols["task_index"]):
+            if ti != prev:
+                start = {side: [round(float(state[i]), 4) for i in ids] for side, ids in idx.items()}
+                start["episode"] = f"{dataset.name}/{f.stem}/seg{seg}"
+                out.append((tasks[ti], start))
+                prev, seg = ti, seg + 1
+    return out
+
+
+def main(argv: list[str]) -> int:
+    if len(argv) < 2:
+        print(__doc__)
+        return 2
+    by: dict[str, list[dict]] = {}
+    for d in argv[1:]:
+        for task, start in segment_starts(Path(d)):
+            by.setdefault(task, []).append(start)
+    Path(argv[0]).write_text(json.dumps({"by_prompt": by}, indent=1) + "\n", encoding="utf-8")
+    for task, starts in by.items():
+        print(f"{len(starts):3d}  {task}")
+    return 0
+
+
+if __name__ == "__main__":
+    raise SystemExit(main(sys.argv[1:]))
diff --git a/tools/trace_report.py b/tools/trace_report.py
